- **repo-guards**: The cross-repo layer, and the only track that is always on. It refuses a write to a path another repo declared as its own, refuses `commit -a` and `push` into a repo this session is not standing in — and when it allows a foreign write, hands back that repo's git status so the blind spot closes. At the end of a turn it names what was left open elsewhere. `close.py` then reports every place at once and commits only what this session actually wrote.
